<a id="b01"></a>
## B01 — Qualified local runtime resources at Start and Switch

Current capability: [R12 — 合格资源、受管维护与显式修复](../R12_PLAN.md#resources). Historical source: `01-qualified-resource.md`.

### Capability result

Establish the first-consumer resource qualification result for actual engine Start/Switch without a managed catalog or release prerequisite.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Use existing saved-profile selection and explicit Start/Restart/Switch. Capture actual executable/model/config paths, source/origin, version, digest, backend and capability compatibility for the selected revision. Unknown provenance is represented honestly and never trusted-ready; local qualified resources are allowed regardless of Releases origin. Saving/selecting a profile or refreshing metadata never starts a Run. Missing/incompatible Start remains No-engine; failed Switch B preserves captured Ready A, not an alternate backend. Validate actual launched companions and dynamic dependencies; the managed schema2 static-zlib exception applies only to an exact unmodified catalog/manifest match. External/official/unknown and modified resources retain their own DLL requirements. Record qualified result by resource revision and invalidate it on same-path content replacement. No implicit download, engine fallback, custom-command rewrite or engine compilation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Valid qualified local executable/model/config can start with origin/version/path/digest and admitted capability displayed; no development-machine fixed path.
- Missing paths, corrupt models, wrong versions/digests/origin and missing/incorrect dependent libraries fail visibly, identify actual failed executable and preserve current game and Ready A.
- Schema2 exact static-zlib exception cannot be borrowed by external/official/unknown or modified executables; CUDA/cuDNN/NVRTC and TensorRT parser failures are attributed correctly.
- Same-path replacement invalidates old validation; old validation completion cannot admit a changed selection or different Run.
- A qualified-resource completion record separates repository, actual compatible-engine/native results, hardware gaps and Not run component signature/installation obligations.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Evidence, native gaps and invalidation

Future owner runs minimal owner/fixture/protocol/state/persistence checks for changed observable boundaries, then the actual admitted local engine and applicable native UI/OS surface. Renderer-only features require actual supported input/layout native proof, not unrelated engine execution. A browser/fixture is not a Windows/native/real-engine/service pass.

Inherited aliases below are original candidate/conditions, never verification on a new candidate. Use only the relevant exact row. Changes to behavior, DTO/protocol, Run/job/document identity, assets/version, platform or service invalidate corresponding inheritance and require affected owner revalidation with stage acceptance observation. Unrelated rows do not add dependencies or reruns. Installed packages, actual GPU/remote endpoints and engine compilation remain independent obligations when applicable; missing hardware/credentials keep only that surface blocked.

<a id="b02"></a>
## B02 — Freeze managed catalog and explicit resource-maintenance admission

Current capability: [R12 — 合格资源、受管维护与显式修复](../R12_PLAN.md#resources). Historical source: `02-catalog-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Produce the missing managed-resource product admission contract, without changing the frozen qualified-local-resource boundary.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: Which managed catalog targets and explicit maintenance UI are admitted, with what immutable trust/default/resource transaction contract?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Freeze catalog origin/source commit/release tag/target/archive/executable/model/config identity, admitted platform/backend/hardware set, explicit maintenance actions, compatibility and offline behavior. Retain all frozen 15 source targets as enumerated sources, not 15 automatically certified supported environments. Decide the specific managed catalog and user repair surface, not production repository, credentials, release signing or channels. Keep public GPU guidance tied to selected resource version; RTX30/40/50 CUDA preference, TRT RTX20/GTX16 and GTX10 rejection are source-version guidance, not permanent universal hardware law. Decide default B11-11750M adoption or non-adoption with named version/reason; active custom selection stays untouched.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Contract enumerates exact managed identity/trust and manifest checks, unsafe archive/path traversal/same-ID forgery rejection and source-version guidance.
- Entry/default/persistence, maintenance start/progress/success/failure/cancel, retain-last-good and stale operation behavior are specified without arbitrary platforms or thresholds.
- Default B11 source/model digest and adoption rationale are explicit; no default upgrade retargets a custom active selection.
- Outputs identify B03 blocked managed implementation and B01 independently deliverable local qualification; no production Release/compile gate is added.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-11`, `REL-05`.

##### T04-RESOURCE

唯一owner `T01-RESOURCE`；B首个资源消费者；跨批共享合同；性质 功能增量（复用T01-RESOURCE；不复制别名）。

- **kind**: 功能增量（复用T01-RESOURCE；不复制别名）
- **batch / owner**: B；随受影响consumer提前；REL-05功能部分/ENG-01原profile边界；签名安装仍T01-RELEASE。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-001、UD-04-002、UD-04-005、UD-04-006、UD-04-008、UD-04-010、UD-04-014、UD-04-016、UD-04-018、UD-04-021、UD-04-024、UD-04-026、UD-04-028、UD-04-035、UD-04-037、UD-04-038、UD-04-043。
- **actual blockers**: 需要源/版本/路径/digest及兼容adapter能力的明确准入合同；可用合格本地资源。具体managed catalog/受控修复产品界面须先冻结；无生产Release/上游编译/签名开工前置。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
1. 原profile可继续用本地资源，不自动获取或切profile。目录资源按origin、source commit、backend、archive/executable与model/config身份验证；schema2的verified managed static-zlib例外只对catalog及manifest精确匹配且未修改的可执行文件适用。external/official/unknown不能借receipt去掉自身dynamic DLL合同，modified拒绝trusted-ready。
2. 15目标来源可枚举但按实际准入平台/硬件获取真实能力，不用CPU/fixture替GPU认证；源build、cloud transfer、JRE/installer实现不迁移。新版engineReleaseTag只改变来源联接，不证明已发布/URL可下载。离线/缺失/不兼容失败可见，既存current-game/ready A保留，autoload失败不fallback。
3. TensorRT repair只由明确用户动作触发，重新核对原选择目标/资源identity，不静默转CUDA/OpenCL；GPU提示不冒称真实GPU认证。保存/选择/刷新不隐式启动引擎。
4. B11-11750M默认变化记录实际采纳/未采纳原因与版本证据；不以default upgrade覆盖custom active selection。model/setup后台refresh失败不误enable，busy动作不可重入，旧snapshot不能覆盖当前identity。
- **验证环境/责任**: 以后用本地qualified binary/model、source fixture与真实已准入引擎/平台；组件签名/安装路径单独Not run。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: 每个上述resource子合同都有来源、失败断言与责任，consumer功能证据可引用；未准入平台保持有名缺口，不能用catalog存在结束。

###### UD-03-010a — 发布脚本产生的公开GPU兼容/下载说明
- **来源 PR / 完整提交**: `1f37094360690ac59eefb038067beeaf38d90d84`, `2f00081d1cff16a3625a4b1c44078b375bf28fdf`。PR和最早发布/full包含见§6/§2；小写子记录来源不超出03区间。
- **行为 / 源码边界**: scripts/r2_release.py 的catalog_label/render_index输出用户指南：RTX30/40/50优先CUDA通常更快，TensorRT仅建议RTX20/GTX16，GTX10不支持，分卷都需下载。这是该来源资源版本的公开指导，不是所有未来TRT版本永久硬件规律。
- **对等项 / 意图**: REL-05（Partial）；T01-RESOURCE；010资格调查关联；`T03-RESOURCE-PUBLIC-GPU-GUIDANCE`。
- **现状 / 处置 / kind**: 未将本新目标记Covered；需后继功能/修复；kind=`feature`。
- **实际阻塞**: 所准入资源版本/平台/硬件compatibility表；没有对应GPU保留待验，不要求当前compile/release。
- **有界可观察断言 / stop**: 未来资源获取/帮助文案与所选binary版本资格一致，不推荐已知不支持项；unknown明确；源指南以对应版本记录，新版变化须重新来源核实。发布脚本技术保留EXC-005。
- **唯一批次 / 责任**: B（资源帮助）；`T03-RESOURCE-PUBLIC-GPU-GUIDANCE`；子记录与本体由06去重为一个相关能力，不能当独立ParityID。

###### UD-03-035a — production修复下载的可信origin/identity/asset边界
- **来源 PR / 完整提交**: `2061865fce2e9ec4a44633fe86f1dd6bde9112d2`, `4019a7e15aa4deaf2a1e5b6e49ae691f1f7db74d`, `e35b01c14e2fb423f09de05311bb68dd1f6d1c1c`。PR和最早发布/full包含见§6/§2；小写子记录来源不超出03区间。
- **行为 / 源码边界**: KataGoAssetCatalog.assetDownloadUrl消费可信catalog：非catalog同ID伪asset拒绝；official-origin不得改download repository，project-source-build origin只准来源允许仓库/完整sourcecommit/合法release tag；source archive名含commit前缀+target，archive名安全。上界KataGoRuntimeHelper:167–170,6196–6199用于TRT/CUDA companion repair，不是仅构建script。Next资源发布仓库待决，不机械硬编码Java仓库。
- **对等项 / 意图**: REL-05（Partial）；T01-RESOURCE / T01-NETWORK；008/010/035相关资源功能；`T03-RESOURCE-TRUSTED-ORIGIN-IDENTITY`。
- **现状 / 处置 / kind**: 未将本新目标记Covered；需后继功能/修复；kind=`feature`。
- **实际阻塞**: T01-RESOURCE集中可信origin/版本/路径/完整性/capability合同及渠道未决；合格本地binary允许；正式签名发行独立。
- **有界可观察断言 / stop**: 不可信origin、不可变identity不全、同ID篡改asset、目录穿越/不安全archive名在下载/安装前可见拒绝；合法本地/可信catalog资源可准入；digest/兼容失败不更改有效资源。选择network policy不能静默Direct。EXC-007仍排除compile/packaging技术。
- **唯一批次 / 责任**: B（及D/C资源消费者）；`T03-RESOURCE-TRUSTED-ORIGIN-IDENTITY`；子记录与本体由06去重为一个相关能力，不能当独立ParityID。

<a id="b03"></a>
## B03 — Managed resource maintenance and version-qualified GPU guidance

Current capability: [R12 — 合格资源、受管维护与显式修复](../R12_PLAN.md#resources). Historical source: `03-managed-resources.md`.

### Capability result

Implement the admitted managed resource acquisition/maintenance surface and version-aware guidance.

### Functional input scope

`B01`, `B02`

The named required decision/admission result is not yet supplied. Known invariants below are mandatory; entry/default/protocol/storage choices delegated to that result remain unresolved, not invented.

### User behavior and boundaries

Only explicit user acquisition/maintenance changes resources; recheck original selected target and identity before commit. Origin/source commit/backend/archive/executable/model/config and digest checks precede install; unsafe/forged catalogs and unsupported policy fail visibly. Each operation has one start, phase sequence and terminal success/failure/cancel. Old operations/snapshots cannot replace new identity. Failure/cancel keeps last-known-good resource and current game/Ready A. Saving/selecting/refreshing does not autostart. Catalog/engineReleaseTag is source linkage, not proof that a release/download exists. Busy gates cover refresh and queued callbacks; refresh failure cannot enable unavailable actions. TRT-specific mutation/repair is outside this base-maintenance ticket and is owned by B73 after repair/hardware decisions; this source contract still identifies unsupported/unknown TRT targets and does not silently CUDA/OpenCL fallback. Download transport consumes C T01-NETWORK when actual network acquisition is admitted; qualified local functionality does not wait for it.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Implement all admitted catalog/maintenance entry/default/save/failure/cancel outcomes from B02 with immutable operation identity.
- Offline/unavailable/wrong-origin/digest/incompatible selection preserves original valid resource and Ready A; no implicit alternate profile or backend.
- Public GPU guidance matches selected binary version and unknown qualification is visible; no fixture/CPU result claims GPU certification.
- Old refresh/maintenance completion cannot retarget selection or report success after cancellation; busy actions are non-reentrant.
- B11 default change follows recorded source adoption without replacing custom active selection; formal signed distribution remains Not run.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T01-RESOURCE`.

Source intents: `T03-RESOURCE-MAINTENANCE-LIFECYCLE`, `T03-REL05-B11-MODEL-UPDATE`, `T03-RESOURCE-PUBLIC-GPU-GUIDANCE`, `T03-RESOURCE-TRUSTED-ORIGIN-IDENTITY`, `T04-RESOURCE`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-11`, `REL-05`.

Delta sources: `UD-03-006`, `UD-03-010A`, `UD-03-035A`, `UD-03-037`, `UD-04-001`, `UD-04-002`, `UD-04-005`, `UD-04-006`, `UD-04-008`, `UD-04-010`, `UD-04-014`, `UD-04-016`, `UD-04-018`, `UD-04-021`, `UD-04-024`, `UD-04-026`, `UD-04-028`, `UD-04-035`, `UD-04-037`, `UD-04-038`, `UD-04-043`.

- **kind**: 功能增量（复用T01-RESOURCE；不复制别名）
- **batch / owner**: B；随受影响consumer提前；REL-05功能部分/ENG-01原profile边界；签名安装仍T01-RELEASE。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-001、UD-04-002、UD-04-005、UD-04-006、UD-04-008、UD-04-010、UD-04-014、UD-04-016、UD-04-018、UD-04-021、UD-04-024、UD-04-026、UD-04-028、UD-04-035、UD-04-037、UD-04-038、UD-04-043。
- **actual blockers**: 需要源/版本/路径/digest及兼容adapter能力的明确准入合同；可用合格本地资源。具体managed catalog/受控修复产品界面须先冻结；无生产Release/上游编译/签名开工前置。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
1. 原profile可继续用本地资源，不自动获取或切profile。目录资源按origin、source commit、backend、archive/executable与model/config身份验证；schema2的verified managed static-zlib例外只对catalog及manifest精确匹配且未修改的可执行文件适用。external/official/unknown不能借receipt去掉自身dynamic DLL合同，modified拒绝trusted-ready。
2. 15目标来源可枚举但按实际准入平台/硬件获取真实能力，不用CPU/fixture替GPU认证；源build、cloud transfer、JRE/installer实现不迁移。新版engineReleaseTag只改变来源联接，不证明已发布/URL可下载。离线/缺失/不兼容失败可见，既存current-game/ready A保留，autoload失败不fallback。
3. TensorRT repair只由明确用户动作触发，重新核对原选择目标/资源identity，不静默转CUDA/OpenCL；GPU提示不冒称真实GPU认证。保存/选择/刷新不隐式启动引擎。
4. B11-11750M默认变化记录实际采纳/未采纳原因与版本证据；不以default upgrade覆盖custom active selection。model/setup后台refresh失败不误enable，busy动作不可重入，旧snapshot不能覆盖当前identity。
- **验证环境/责任**: 以后用本地qualified binary/model、source fixture与真实已准入引擎/平台；组件签名/安装路径单独Not run。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: 每个上述resource子合同都有来源、失败断言与责任，consumer功能证据可引用；未准入平台保持有名缺口，不能用catalog存在结束。

Future owner runs minimal owner/fixture/protocol/state/persistence checks for changed observable boundaries, then the actual admitted local engine and applicable native UI/OS surface. Unrelated rows do not add dependencies or reruns. <a id="b04"></a>
## B04 — Determine ownership of target-directed TensorRT repair

Current capability: [R12 — 合格资源、受管维护与显式修复](../R12_PLAN.md#resources). Historical source: `04-trt-investigation.md`.

### Capability result

Decide whether admitted resources own TRT-directed repair, external responsibility, or a specific product decision.

### Functional input scope

`B01`

### User behavior and boundaries

Use frozen resource acquisition/replacement boundaries, not new Java diagnosis. Compare selectable TRT identity to maintenance ownership even with non-TRT foreground engine. Stop at supported/external-owner/needs-decision table and exact retained-resource failure/cancel contract. No repair implementation before this result; a supported repair successor must be linked to managed-resources and retain last-known-good, original user target and explicit action. No new engine compile.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Every admitted TRT target has named repair owner and explicit supported/external/decision result.
- Result defines target identity, user action, stale/cancel handling and failure-retain-last-good boundaries.
- No fix is issued merely because the Java Delta exists; unsupported product choice has a precise blocker and owner.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

<a id="b05"></a>
## B05 — Bound NVIDIA hardware qualification for admitted repair targets

Current capability: [R12 — 合格资源、受管维护与显式修复](../R12_PLAN.md#resources). Historical source: `05-nvidia-investigation.md`.

### Capability result

Produce supported/unsupported/unknown hardware qualification and evidence responsibility for each admitted target.

### Functional input scope

`B04`

### User behavior and boundaries

Compare target metadata to actual available probe and hardware evidence, including a non-TRT foreground selection. Unknown or absent GPU remains pending, never pass. Determine whether resource repair needs NVIDIA qualification and transaction rollback from the TRT ownership result; do not broaden to general engine builds or all GPUs.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Metadata/probe matrix distinguishes supported, unsupported, unknown with actual hardware/evidence owner.
- Missing GPU cannot be replaced by CPU/fixture/catalog existence; wrong qualification cannot install or destroy last-good.
- Repair contract specifies target revalidation and resource rollback; further implementation is linked only if admitted.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### User behavior and boundaries

Consume which setup/repair actions were retained, not a new Java layout architecture. Display actionable actual target state, grouped controls and complete labels. In unsupported/unknown/busy states actions explain limits rather than enabling impossible work. Settings entry does not launch an engine. Reuse A resource-key/locale/fallback result only, not all global search. Do not invent fixed 600px/DPI matrices; implementation records real supported window/work-area/language risks.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- All retained actions/status/labels remain reachable at the UI actual supported work-area/DPI/language boundaries.
- Keyboard/focus and scroll reach all actions; unknown/busy/failure states are readable without hidden controls.
- UI does not auto-run, retarget profile or claim uncertified hardware.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Evidence, native gaps and invalidation

Future owner runs minimal owner/fixture/protocol/state/persistence checks for changed observable boundaries, then the actual admitted local engine and applicable native UI/OS surface. Renderer-only features require actual supported input/layout native proof, not unrelated engine execution. A browser/fixture is not a Windows/native/real-engine/service pass.

<a id="b07"></a>
## B07 — Map model headers, ownership and installed retention

Current capability: [R12 — 模型身份与已安装候选保留](../R12_PLAN.md#models). Historical source: `07-model-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver a bounded source/local-sample inventory and technical model-header/ownership/catalog-retention contract for B08.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Technical output: named header formats and local samples, parsing/failure representation, content/catalog/selection identities and installed-retention mapping implementing the frozen assertions.

This is executor-owned source/sample and technical design work within the frozen behavior, not unresolved product approval. Only a specifically evidenced unsupported case, proposed non-equivalent cut or source/ADR conflict needs an item-specific disposition; it does not block unaffected behavior.

### User behavior and boundaries

Inspect frozen source and available local samples for supported header formats and unknown/corrupt handling; distinguish content identity, catalog metadata and saved profile selection. Profile name is never ownership. Map retention across B11→B10→B11, same-path replacement validation, snapshot refresh/busy and manual-command/rename/restart ownership preservation. No invented model-strength guarantee or custom candidate disappearance. An unsupported format is a named bounded evidence gap, not permission to drop custom candidates.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Named header formats/samples and unknown/corrupt behavior are recorded, with model/source/path identity.
- Managed ownership survives rename/manual-command/restart, never overwrites custom inputs.
- Retention/catalog contract keeps installed candidates available across model changes without repeated download.
- Result specifies entry/default/save/cancel/refresh failure and stale identity, unlocking B08 only.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T04-MODEL-IDENTITY`.

Source intents: `T04-MODEL-IDENTITY`, `T05-MODEL-IDENTITY`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-11`, `ENG-01`.

Delta sources: `UD-04-030`, `UD-04-031`, `UD-04-039`, `UD-04-043`, `UD-05-05`.

<a id="b08"></a>
## B08 — Model identity and installed-candidate retention

Current capability: [R12 — 模型身份与已安装候选保留](../R12_PLAN.md#models). Historical source: `08-model-identity.md`.

### Capability result

Expose real model identity and preserve installed choices independently of display-name and catalog refresh.

### Functional input scope

`B01`, `B07`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

Ordinary scoped predecessors supply B07 header/sample/identity/retention technical mapping and B01 qualification; source-answerable details and engineering representation are executor-owned, not unresolved product choices. Consume A's validated locale/resource-key/fallback result only. no complete diagnostics suite or installed support package is a prerequisite.

### User behavior and boundaries

Read admitted actual model headers in background and display true source/version/path; unknown/corrupt is visible, no fabricated strength and no disappearance of custom candidate. Same-path replacement revalidates content. Keep original origin through profile rename/manual command/restart. Immutable refresh snapshots/busy gate preserve saved selection, failures do not enable. B11→B10→B11 uses installed candidates, not download-only/re-download. No engine available still permits normal review.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Header/content/catalog/selection identities match admitted model samples and failure cases.
- Rename/manual command/restart does not change managed ownership or rewrite custom inputs.
- B11→B10→B11 retains installed availability; refresh failure/late callback cannot retarget current selection.
- Actual compatible local model/engine selection is recorded; download, signed package and engine compilation are separate surfaces.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T04-MODEL-IDENTITY`.

Source intents: `T04-MODEL-IDENTITY`, `T05-MODEL-IDENTITY`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-11`, `ENG-01`.

Delta sources: `UD-04-030`, `UD-04-031`, `UD-04-039`, `UD-04-043`, `UD-05-05`.

#### Frozen source behavior contracts

Each source retains its complete original requirement below. This source covers only its stated scope/decision; current capability routes own other parts of a shared source. Source feature assertions accompanying a decision are requirements for its successor, not commands to implement during the investigation.

##### T04-MODEL-IDENTITY

唯一owner `T04-MODEL-IDENTITY`；B；ENG-01原profile；T01-RESOURCE；未分配扩展。；性质 本地resource/model用户功能后继。

- **kind**: 本地resource/model用户功能后继
- **batch / owner**: B；ENG-01原profile；T01-RESOURCE；未分配扩展。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-030、UD-04-031、UD-04-039、UD-04-043。
- **actual blockers**: 模型header、managed ownership、retention/catalog snapshot的产品合同与可用本地样本；不以RCOMP-01/ENG-03占位。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
解析header获得真实模型identity，损坏/unknown可见且custom候选不消失；同路径replacement重新validate。profile名称≠ownership，rename/manual command/restart不丢原origin或覆写custom inputs。B11→B10→B11都保持installed候选，不变为download-only、不需重复下载；refresh后台snapshot+busy gate不retarget saved identity、失败不误enable。
- **验证环境/责任**: header/catalog/persistence fixtures与合格本地真实模型/引擎选择；网络下载仅若consumer实现需真实统一策略，不签名Release。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: metadata/content/selection identity和installed availability的每条断言有证据；原profile存储Accepted不扩大。

完整行为/非目标/实际阻塞/证据门：[UD-04-030](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-030), [UD-04-031](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-031), [UD-04-039](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-039), [UD-04-043](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-043).

Future owner runs minimal owner/fixture/protocol/state/persistence checks for changed observable boundaries, then the actual admitted local engine and applicable native UI/OS surface. Renderer-only features require actual supported input/layout native proof, not unrelated engine execution. A browser/fixture is not a Windows/native/real-engine/service pass.

Inherited aliases below are original candidate/conditions, never verification on a new candidate. Use only the relevant exact row. Changes to behavior, DTO/protocol, Run/job/document identity, assets/version, platform or service invalidate corresponding inheritance and require affected owner revalidation with stage acceptance observation. Unrelated rows do not add dependencies or reruns. Installed packages, actual GPU/remote endpoints and engine compilation remain independent obligations when applicable; missing hardware/credentials keep only that surface blocked.

<a id="b09"></a>
## B09 — Define bounded first-consumer diagnostics and export budgets

Current capability: [R12 — 首消费者诊断与响应式控制台](../R12_PLAN.md#diagnostics). Historical source: `09-diagnostics-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Make the frozen diagnostics behavior executable by naming actual roles, source limits and collection/export deadlines.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Technical output: rationale-backed source limits, available native collection roles, truthful missing metadata/metrics and bounded queue/shutdown/cancellable-export design preserving the frozen privacy and retention behavior.

This is executor-owned source/sample and technical design work within the frozen behavior, not unresolved product approval. Only a specifically evidenced unsupported case, proposed non-equivalent cut or source/ADR conflict needs an item-specific disposition; it does not block unaffected behavior.

### User behavior and boundaries

Preserve required logs, per-attempt identity, redaction, cancellable bounded export, full trace off and no auto-upload. Determine native collectable task/thread/runtime metrics and truthful missing representation; source/risk-backed per-source bytes/lines/time/queue bounds, shutdown/export stage bounds, supported metadata/file identity and safe retention implement the frozen contract. Numerical engineering budgets and native API representation are executor-owned, not new collection/privacy policy approval. Limits require source/risk rationale, not arbitrary last50/1000 lines/Windows latency promise. One frozen export request pins displayed error and stable per-bundle pseudonyms across files. PR545 final scope is visible per-attempt failure/command/details/copy/pinned export, NOT removed PEscanner or universal launcher session history.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Budget table names source, bytes/lines/deadline, queue/admission/cancel/terminal behavior and rationale; no unlimited recovery or I/O.
- Native collection roles/units/time/missing and platform metadata/unknown identity handling are explicit.
- Privacy contract covers tokens/room/session IDs/URLs/user paths/control characters and multiline record boundaries with consistent bundle aliases.
- Pinned displayed-attempt export, atomic publish, stage failures/cleanup/open-folder-after-success and shutdown missing semantics are defined.
- Implementation B10 and each C/D/E consumer can reuse result; installed log/support package remains R11-owned.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`.

Source intents: `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T03-REL09-DIAG-BUNDLE-EXPORT`, `T03-REL09-THREAD-SNAPSHOT`, `T03-ENG01-BOOTSTRAP-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T03-REL09-RUNTIME-SNAPSHOT`, `T03-ENG01-GTP-PROBE-STDERR`, `T03-REL09-LOGGING-GENERATION`, `T03-REL09-ANALYSIS-CACHE-TRACE`, `T03-REL09-SANITIZER-BOUNDARIES`, `T03-REL09-EXPORT-BOUNDED-IO`, `T03-ENG01-ISOLATED-STARTUP-FAILURE`, `T03-REL09-SHUTDOWN-CLEANUP`, `T04-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T05-STARTUP-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T05-SECRET-DIAGNOSTICS`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `REL-09`, `ENG-07`.

Delta sources: `UD-03-001`, `UD-03-002`, `UD-03-003`, `UD-03-004`, `UD-03-005`, `UD-03-019`, `UD-03-025`, `UD-03-030`, `UD-03-031`, `UD-03-042`, `UD-03-052`, `UD-04-040`, `UD-04-046`, `UD-04-048`, `UD-05-08`, `UD-05-10`.

##### T04-DIAGNOSTICS

唯一owner `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`；B（首个Run/资源消费者）；C/D/E各消费者扩展；R11仅安装支持包；性质 功能增量（T01-DIAGNOSTICS/REL-09功能部分）。

- **kind**: 功能增量（T01-DIAGNOSTICS/REL-09功能部分）
- **batch / owner**: 随引擎/分析等受影响功能交付，非统一等E/R11；REL-09 Missing原scope；ENG-02原typed failure。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-040、UD-04-046、UD-04-048。
- **actual blockers**: 需要bounded attempt/output/export identity与redaction合同；相关启动路径真实证据以后取得，不以PEscanner为prereq。安装日志路径/support package另gate。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
最终PR545只有per-attempt visible failure/command、details、copy、pinned displayed-error export；普通WARN、late stdout/stderr保留原engine/launch identity，有限bytes/lines/deadline，不把新Run输出接到旧attempt。Copy/export通过同redaction，包含明显source/status/exit与缺失可见，control chars/credential/token不得泄漏。export受限可取消，close/retry/switch不改变被导出的displayed snapshot，failure可见且不自动上传/fulltrace。新diag窗口长文本/buttons在实际Windowswork-area/DPI可达；PEscanner/所有internal launcher统一session-history已删除，不列最终migration功能。
- **验证环境/责任**: source/fixtures与真实Startup error/window/导出；Linux/browser不是Windowsnative。功能资源可本地，installed日志路径单列。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: 每个受影响功能已有必要可见错误/日志/可取消脱敏导出门，最终source cutover和对应native残余不丢。

完整行为/非目标/实际阻塞/证据门：[UD-04-040](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-040), [UD-04-046](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-046), [UD-04-048](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-048).

###### UD-03-001 — 诊断入口、启动失败回退与支持指引
- **核心文件出处**: `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/bug_report.yml`, `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/installation_report.yml`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, `SUPPORT.md` 等共 5 文件
- **用户可见行为与边界**: 帮助菜单导出脱敏诊断包；无法启动时有可定位的普通/崩溃日志回退。不是新增工单平台。
- **对等项 / 任务映射**: REL-09（Missing）；T01-DIAGNOSTICS；`T03-REL09-DIAG-BUNDLE-EXPORT`。所有历史项仅引用Matrix原范围，最终后继ID由06分配。
- **Tauri 现状 / 证据与残余**: 基线没有 Help 诊断导出入口或支持包 UI。
- **处置结论**: 需后继功能/修复。任务 kind=`feature`；不把Source差异等同Next缺陷。
- **功能断言 / 调查停止条件**: 导出或取消有可见结果；ZIP 内秘密不出现；无法启动时指引能定位实际回退日志。
- **实际阻塞与未来证据门**: T01-DIAGNOSTICS 的功能日志/脱敏合同；原生目录与正式安装路径分别验收。
- **唯一批次建议 / 责任**: B/C/D 随消费者；公共入口 E；`T03-REL09-DIAG-BUNDLE-EXPORT` 拥有此Delta的结果。具体合同按来源保留，同源内容去重不改变结果责任。

###### UD-03-002 — 有界线程/任务快照与包内敏感身份别名
- **核心文件出处**: `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/Lizzie.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/logging/DiagnosticBundleExporter.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/logging/EdtHangWatchdog.java`, `src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/logging/ThreadSnapshot.java`
- **用户可见行为与边界**: 快照保留 threadId= 的数字线程标识、线程状态/栈/角色；采集失败不破坏支持包。线程标识不等于弈客 room/session 身份。后者同一 ZIP 内只使用稳定 route-kind+序号别名，不能导出原值或可枚举数字哈希。

###### UD-04-046 最终startup failure、bounded output、WARN与pinned export
- **Source / event**: PR #545 (集成事件 38) | 共 14 个提交；第一父E38。
- **实际behavior / sourceDelta**: PR545保留全部14来源，包括早期fedeb66c的PeDependencyScanner/PeImportReader和独立launcher/history接入；7f2dc02f最后删除这两production classes及tests，移除internal probes/session-history集成。最终是per-attempt GUI error/command、Windows status/explicit DLL/runtime evidence、bounded stdout/stderr tail、ordinary WARN原engine+launch identity、details/copy及导出当前displayed failure。不是PE扫描/全launcher历史保留功能。
- **Canonical owner / scope**: REL-09 Missing；T01-DIAGNOSTICS；ENG-02原typed failures。
- **候选/覆盖与边界**: ENG-02原typed error范围保留；REL-09仍Missing，已有chrome错误不等于普通日志/详情/导出全部实现。
- **Disposition**: 需诊断功能后继；中间机制显式撤回。
- **责任意图**: T04-DIAGNOSTICS。
- **Observable assertions**: 原attempt/engine/launch身份绑定，后到output不重定向new run；bounded lines/bytes/deadline与取消可见；retry/switch后打开旧failure导出必须pin displayed snapshot，不混当前错误；普通WARN与Copy/export均redact secrets/control chars、来源明确，受限可取消导出失败可见。不必复制已删除PE scanner，不做自动upload。

<a id="b10"></a>
## B10 — First-consumer bounded runtime diagnostics and pinned export

Current capability: [R12 — 首消费者诊断与响应式控制台](../R12_PLAN.md#diagnostics). Historical source: `10-diagnostics.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver required startup/resource/analysis diagnostics at their first actual Run consumer, not after release.

### Functional input scope

`B09`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

Ordinary scoped predecessors supply B09 bounded budget/collection/privacy/export technical result and B01 qualification at the first actual Run/resource consumer; source-answerable details and engineering representation are executor-owned, not unresolved product choices. Consume A's validated locale/resource-key/fallback result only. no complete diagnostics suite or installed support package is a prerequisite.

### User behavior and boundaries

Bound ordinary WARN/stdout/stderr/log queues and attribute them to original engine/launch/Run/attempt; late output never attaches to a new attempt. Distinguish bad dependency/config, incomplete probe EOF, timeout and process exit. Visible current failure details/copy/export share sanitizer; missing data/source/status/exit remain explicit. Native thread/task/runtime snapshots use only available real metrics with units/time, missing items do not kill bundle. Full trace defaults off; explicit trace adoption/rejection reasons match actual node publication and trace failures never block results. Freeze displayed-attempt export at request; retry/switch/close does not retarget it. Export is bounded/cancellable, maintains multiline records and cross-file aliases, safely prunes only proven old identities; unknown/new same-path file identities are retained. No partial output reported as success, but folder-open failure after atomic publish retains export success. Recovery generations do not revive old pending writes; shutdown drains or reports missing within budget, force kill never claims durable flush.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- All frozen diagnostics sources produce actual bounded success/failure/cancel states at real first startup/resource consumer, not an unused framework.
- Fake secrets with spaces/quotes/newlines/URLs/fallback argv do not leak via screen, ordinary logs, copy or ZIP; record boundaries and aliases stay consistent.
- Delayed A failure cannot overwrite successful B or Match; retry/close/new Run cannot change pinned displayed-error export.
- Metrics missing, collection failure, export estimate generation, cancellation/close/force failure, safe pruning and post-publish folder failure match B09 contract.
- Trace off emits no cache-adoption line; trace on reasons match accepted/rejected stronger/weaker/ownership/invalid-identity results.
- Normal/interrupted shutdown is bounded without Run resurrection; actual Windows work-area/DPI long-text controls are reachable; installed-path support bundle remains Not run.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`.

Source intents: `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T03-REL09-DIAG-BUNDLE-EXPORT`, `T03-REL09-THREAD-SNAPSHOT`, `T03-ENG01-BOOTSTRAP-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T03-REL09-RUNTIME-SNAPSHOT`, `T03-ENG01-GTP-PROBE-STDERR`, `T03-REL09-LOGGING-GENERATION`, `T03-REL09-ANALYSIS-CACHE-TRACE`, `T03-REL09-SANITIZER-BOUNDARIES`, `T03-REL09-EXPORT-BOUNDED-IO`, `T03-ENG01-ISOLATED-STARTUP-FAILURE`, `T03-REL09-SHUTDOWN-CLEANUP`, `T04-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T05-STARTUP-DIAGNOSTICS`, `T05-SECRET-DIAGNOSTICS`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `REL-09`, `ENG-07`, `ENG-02`, `ENG-04`.

Delta sources: `UD-03-001`, `UD-03-002`, `UD-03-003`, `UD-03-004`, `UD-03-005`, `UD-03-019`, `UD-03-025`, `UD-03-030`, `UD-03-031`, `UD-03-042`, `UD-03-052`, `UD-04-040`, `UD-04-046`, `UD-04-048`, `UD-05-08`, `UD-05-10`.

##### T03-REL09-SHUTDOWN-CLEANUP

唯一owner `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`；B（首个Run/资源消费者）；C/D/E各消费者扩展；R11仅安装支持包；性质 feature。

完整行为/非目标/实际阻塞/证据门：[UD-03-052](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-03-052).

##### T04-DIAGNOSTICS

唯一owner `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`；B（首个Run/资源消费者）；C/D/E各消费者扩展；R11仅安装支持包；性质 功能增量（T01-DIAGNOSTICS/REL-09功能部分）。

- **kind**: 功能增量（T01-DIAGNOSTICS/REL-09功能部分）
- **batch / owner**: 随引擎/分析等受影响功能交付，非统一等E/R11；REL-09 Missing原scope；ENG-02原typed failure。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-040、UD-04-046、UD-04-048。
- **actual blockers**: 需要bounded attempt/output/export identity与redaction合同；相关启动路径真实证据以后取得，不以PEscanner为prereq。安装日志路径/support package另gate。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
最终PR545只有per-attempt visible failure/command、details、copy、pinned displayed-error export；普通WARN、late stdout/stderr保留原engine/launch identity，有限bytes/lines/deadline，不把新Run输出接到旧attempt。Copy/export通过同redaction，包含明显source/status/exit与缺失可见，control chars/credential/token不得泄漏。export受限可取消，close/retry/switch不改变被导出的displayed snapshot，failure可见且不自动上传/fulltrace。新diag窗口长文本/buttons在实际Windowswork-area/DPI可达；PEscanner/所有internal launcher统一session-history已删除，不列最终migration功能。
- **验证环境/责任**: source/fixtures与真实Startup error/window/导出；Linux/browser不是Windowsnative。功能资源可本地，installed日志路径单列。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: 每个受影响功能已有必要可见错误/日志/可取消脱敏导出门，最终source cutover和对应native残余不丢。

完整行为/非目标/实际阻塞/证据门：[UD-04-040](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-040), [UD-04-046](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-046), [UD-04-048](https://github.com/qiyi71w/lizzieyzy-next-tauri/blob/15b71e8bd89349961b7cf25f2fd2a6928e991e40/docs/UPSTREAM_DELTA.md#ud-04-048).

唯一owner `T01-DIAGNOSTICS`；B（首个Run/资源消费者）；C/D/E各消费者扩展；R11仅安装支持包；性质 see-source-disposition。

- 后续验收/调查停止条件：缺路径/坏配置/版本不兼容/子进程退出均显示真实原因，普通日志有界，敏感值不出屏或导出。

- **功能断言 / 调查停止条件**: 导出或取消有可见结果；ZIP 内秘密不出现；无法启动时指引能定位实际回退日志。

<a id="b13"></a>
## B13 — Decide last-primary-engine startup policy preservation

Current capability: [R12 — 启动、默认引擎、预加载与评估让路](../R12_PLAN.md#startup). Historical source: `13-last-autoload-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Close the named historical non-equivalence with a retained executable behavior contract or item-specific approved disposition.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: Retain which non-equivalent user behavior for Last-primary-engine startup policy, or where is the item-specific approval for each non-equivalent disposition?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Java autoload-last remembers the last primary on normal shutdown; missing last-engine=-1 and corrupt index must not choose another profile. Next selected_profile_id is editing identity, not startup identity; existing zero-or-one Autoload Default remains accepted. Inspect original corresponding disposition ticket and approval source already named in frozen Inventory/audits, not new Java investigation. Missing approval is not permission to delete. Decide Startup mode/default, stable last-run profile identity, normal-shutdown/crash persistence, deleted/corrupt identity and failure no-fallback.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Source-to-user-goal table compares every named retained control/default/persistence/failure to current accepted scope; historical routing is not equivalence proof.
- Retained contract names observable entry/default/save/cancel/failure and exact successor; non-equivalent permanent change carries named approver and item-specific citation.
- Unresolved choices keep linked implementation needs-info; original Accepted item/candidate/range is preserved.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T02-H17`.

Source intents: `T02-H17`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-06`, `ENG-01`.

Delta sources: Frozen baseline/preservation source; no new Delta ID invented.

#### Baseline entries, defaults and persistence

## B14 — Retained last-primary-engine startup policy successor

Current capability: [R12 — 启动、默认引擎、预加载与评估让路](../R12_PLAN.md#startup). Historical source: `14-last-autoload-retained.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver only the approved retained user behavior or evidence-backed no-change closure produced by the preservation decision.

### Functional input scope

`B13`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

The named required decision/admission result is not yet supplied. Known invariants below are mandatory; entry/default/protocol/storage choices delegated to that result remain unresolved, not invented.

### User behavior and boundaries

Java autoload-last remembers the last primary on normal shutdown; missing last-engine=-1 and corrupt index must not choose another profile. Next selected_profile_id is editing identity, not startup identity; existing zero-or-one Autoload Default remains accepted. This source contract is explicitly blocked: Startup mode/default, stable last-run profile identity, normal-shutdown/crash persistence, deleted/corrupt identity and failure no-fallback have not all been frozen. The decision must state the exact missing user behavior; no automatic reproduction of Java machinery, no new semantics assumed. If it establishes unchanged accepted equivalence for a control, close that control with exact inherited evidence rather than duplicate implementation; other retained residuals remain owned here. Scope closes only with a complete per-control decision and actual acceptance, never a silent empty feature.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Decision contract is linked and its retained entries/defaults/persistence/failure/cancel have actual acceptance; unresolved choices do not become ready.
- Failed save/cancel does not apply pending settings or start/switch an engine; old work cannot mutate current game/Run.
- Every original non-equivalent goal is delivered or individually approved and evidenced; no speculative broad Accepted promotion.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### User behavior and boundaries

Java saved non-primary preload defaults false and is not foreground promotion. Next processes currently exist only under Switch/Match Reservation. Preserve preload user goal without approving hidden parallel lifecycle by assumption. This source contract is explicitly blocked: Opt-in entry/default/save, background readiness display, single-manager reservation ownership, cancellation/resource failure and foreground priority have not all been frozen. The decision must state the exact missing user behavior; no automatic reproduction of Java machinery, no new semantics assumed. If it establishes unchanged accepted equivalence for a control, close that control with exact inherited evidence rather than duplicate implementation; other retained residuals remain owned here. Scope closes only with a complete per-control decision and actual acceptance, never a silent empty feature.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

## B43 — Manual dynamic thread Apply and confirmed readback

Current capability: [R12 — 手动运行线程来源、临时覆盖与实际值](../R12_PLAN.md#manual-threads). Historical source: `43-runtime-threads.md`.

### Capability result

Apply explicitly requested temporary thread changes on an admitted current Run and show actual confirmed value.

### Functional input scope

`B42`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

The named required decision/admission result is not yet supplied. Known invariants below are mandatory; entry/default/protocol/storage choices delegated to that result remain unresolved, not invented.

### User behavior and boundaries

Consume B42 policy/protocol result, not benchmark. Mouse/keyboard apply use same action, known capability and captured Run/profile revision; display requested/pending/actual/source separately. Only valid current response confirms value. Invalid/non-finite/protocol error/timeout/old Run response leaves last known valid value with visible unknown/failure, never overwrites newer unsent user edit. Temporary reset/expiry/restart/switch/disconnect follows approved contract and cannot auto-reapply to remote/new Run. Incompatible/static adapter visibly refuses dynamic operation; no automatic engine restart or Java range assumption.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Real admitted engine changes value and confirms actual readback through both actual input paths.
- Errors/timeouts/old Run and replaced request cannot claim success or replace latest user edit.
- Temporary reset/Restart/Switch/disconnect lifetime matches decision; no implicit reapplication/launch override rewrite.
- Demonstrate manual workflow with no benchmark/recommendation and record engine/version/native conditions; static argv remains separate proof.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

Owner intents: `T05-RUNTIME-THREADS`.

Source contract 036/053 supplies policy input; inspect resolved admitted config/include/effective launch, not merely generated key text. Question: does managed numSearchThreadsPerAnalysisThread injection conflict with legacy numSearchThreads? If no injection path, close with exact boundary; if reachable, define precedence for cfg/include both aliases and explicit numSearchThreads/numAnalysisThreads overrides, keeping file bytes intact. Temporary empty alias must not overwrite explicit overrides. HumanSL overlay consumer stays D; static -threads/-search-threads is not this alias claim. This bounded current-path/source comparison does not wait on full benchmark or saved-policy implementation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- No-path proof or exact config/include conflict/precedence table is delivered for each applicable admitted adapter.
- Both alias/config/include and each explicit override are preserved without config byte rewriting.
- Named real-compatible launch evidence requirement and any precise repair successor are produced only if gap exists.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

<a id="b47"></a>
## B47 — Decide startup performance evaluation entry and foreground priority

Current capability: [R12 — 启动、默认引擎、预加载与评估让路](../R12_PLAN.md#startup). Historical source: `47-startup-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Close missing item-specific startup evaluation/default/persistence/yield product policy.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

Decision/investigation output question: Which startup performance evaluation/default/yield policy is retained or item-specifically approved for replacement?

The named choice is unresolved; a bounded decision/result does not constitute prior approval or implementation acceptance.

### User behavior and boundaries

Frozen PR500/504 and preload H18 have retained performance user goal; single-lifecycle constraint is not approval to delete it. Decide explicit entry or admitted startup trigger/default/save behavior, engine selection, user analysis/Pause priority and yield versus true failure feedback. Cite approved individual disposition or retained successor. No actual benchmark required in decision and no Java independent process scheduler migration. This does not block manual Benchmark/Threads or existing current-analysis handoff investigations.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Named entry/trigger/default/persistence/foreground priority/cancel/failure and approval/source are delivered.
- Yield to user analysis/training is not a false failure, extra implicit Run or lost Pause intent.
- B48 implementable retained scope or individually approved no-change result is precise; H18/preload and measured report obligations are not erased.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T04-STARTUP-PERFORMANCE`.

Source intents: `T04-STARTUP-PERFORMANCE`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ENG-15`.

Delta sources: `UD-04-009`, `UD-04-013`.

#### Frozen source behavior contracts

Each source retains its complete original requirement below. This source covers only its stated scope/decision; current capability routes own other parts of a shared source. Source feature assertions accompanying a decision are requirements for its successor, not commands to implement during the investigation.

- **actual blockers**: 缺少逐项startup评估/默认/让位的历史批准和Next对应用户合同；普通single-lifecycle原则不能充当批准。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
保留性能评估用户目标、分析优先、不误报、pause intent与明确引擎选择；调查freeze source的entry/default/persistence/失败，决定显式入口/是否启动评估/如何让位，注明批准人/来源。Java调度可排除但非等价剩余不得静默删。
- **验证环境/责任**: 固定源及approved decision记录；没有真实benchmark义务在本票。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。

- **Canonical owner / scope**: 用户性能目标未分配后继；ANA-06原范围不变。
- **候选/覆盖与边界**: 尚无此新增行为的可继承运行证据；不把Java修复判为当前Next缺陷。
- **Disposition**: Java调度机制排除；用户目标具名决策。
- **责任意图**: T04-STARTUP-PERFORMANCE。
- **Observable assertions**: 先决定性能评估入口/默认/保存/让位边界；产出可判定用户合同及批准引用。保留用户主动分析/暂停优先，无隐式Run/backend；未找到批准时保持具名余项，不闭合为approved replacement。

- **Canonical owner / scope**: ANA-06/16窄范围；未分配性能后继。
- **候选/覆盖与边界**: H-ANALYSIS原Windows范围可引用；无startup性能评估等价证据，不能标全部Covered。
- **Disposition**: 原分析证据窄继承；startup性能决策保留。
- **责任意图**: T04-STARTUP-PERFORMANCE、T04-HANDOFF。
- **Observable assertions**: 按T04-STARTUP-PERFORMANCE冻结显式行为；若重用现分析能力，H-ANALYSIS仅证明有限交还等原范围，不替startup benchmark等价。后台性能工作让位后不得弹伪失败、启动额外隐式Run或覆盖暂停。

No product/native run is required to decide policy. Source/product approval and a decision-complete output are the acceptance surface. Native/engine/protocol evidence needed for implementation is assigned to the blocked feature; no fabricated live result.

<a id="b48"></a>
## B48 — Approved startup evaluation and non-disruptive yield

Current capability: [R12 — 启动、默认引擎、预加载与评估让路](../R12_PLAN.md#startup). Historical source: `48-startup-performance.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver only the approved startup performance behavior and foreground yield policy.

### Functional input scope

`B47`, `B38`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

The named required decision/admission result is not yet supplied. Known invariants below are mandatory; entry/default/protocol/storage choices delegated to that result remain unresolved, not invented.

### User behavior and boundaries

Entry/default/trigger/saved policy is blocked on B47. Retained invariants: explicit saved engine selection, user analysis/training/Pause priority, no implicit Run/backend switch, cancellation non-destructive, yielded work not misreported failed. Use B38 runner results only for admitted evaluation; no automatic measured Apply/config rewriting. If decision approves a narrower replacement, link item-specific approval and delivered user behavior rather than declaring Java scheduling irrelevant.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Every approved entry/default/save/trigger/yield scenario has actual compatible-engine user-visible evidence.
- Analysis/Pause/training starts do not gain false failure dialogs, hidden process switch or stale later reactivation.
- Failed/cancelled startup evaluation preserves previous configuration and current game; measured Apply is still explicit.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

Source intents: `T04-STARTUP-PERFORMANCE`.

## B50 — Ordinary engine rules confirmation and immutable Match consumer result

Current capability: [R12 — 运行生命周期、规则确认、恢复与身份隔离](../R12_PLAN.md#lifecycle). Historical source: `50-rules-lifecycle.md`.

### Capability result

Provide actual ordinary engine instance rules/confirmation and immutable rule-capability result consumed by D Match lifecycle.

### Functional input scope

`B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

### User behavior and boundaries

Query actual current admitted engine instance and bind confirmed rules/komi/capability to Run/position revision; parser RU/KM or a requested value is not engine confirmation. Invalid/error/send failure/timeout/late old Run cannot establish synced/ready. Unsupported capability visibly refuses affected admission without changing current game/rules. Define immutable operational Match snapshot result with fields actually confirmed and adapter capability boundary; D consumes it to freeze participants, admit/restore original rules and show read-only details under its own Match owner. B routes full UD03-029 source and owns ordinary/shared result; D alone implements match consumer/storage/share. No save into personal C or competing generated-text channel. D T03-MATCH-RULES-STORAGE-DECISION blocks only save/share residual, not B ordinary confirmation nor D admission/restoration/read-only detail.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Actual admitted instance success binds same requested rules/komi/current Run/position, negative/error/timeout/late cases fail without false confirmation.
- Immutable shared operational snapshot and capability result is usable by D freeze/admission/restoration/read-only-detail consumer with exact identities.
- D match consumer acceptance covers actual participant compatibility/original-rule restoration; storage decision is scoped to save/share and never added as this prerequisite.
- No personal C rewrite, automatic alternate rules/profile/Run or unsupported success; real local engine/native result is recorded.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

Source intents: `T03-MATCH-RULES-LIFECYCLE`.

- **核心文件出处**: src/main/java/featurecat/lizzie/analysis/KataGoRules.java, EngineRulesResult.java；enginegame/MatchRulesAdmission.java, MatchRulesSnapshot.java, MatchRuleOption.java；对应MatchRulesSnapshotTest.java
- **用户可见行为与边界**: 普通窗口按实例setting/query能力报告pending/confirmed/failed；list_commands只证能力不证实际规则。Match接收时冻结目标和双方身份，overlay前捕获双方original；capability matrix支持confirmed、明确unverified consent及reject。仅exact preset归标准，扩展字段/Custom保留。restore originals成功才release；caption/Shift+D/details/save读取immutable snapshot不查询live引擎。
- **对等项 / 任务映射**: GAME-01（Accepted，既有Match）；具名 match-rules successor；RULE-01仅SGF编辑规则；`T03-MATCH-RULES-LIFECYCLE`。所有历史项仅引用Matrix原范围，最终后继ID由06分配。
- **Tauri 现状 / 证据与残余**: 此规则生命周期/保存详情不在RULE-01或026入口guard历史范围。
- **额外来源 / 继承条件**: fe4af867 body；enginegame/MatchRulesAdmission.java, MatchRulesSnapshotTest.java, MatchRuleOption.java；local准备合同。
- **处置结论**: 需后继功能/修复。任务 kind=`feature`；不把Source差异等同Next缺陷。
- **功能断言 / 调查停止条件**: 双方confirmed目标一致才confirmed准入；可query失败/mismatch/incomplete拒绝，unsupported按明确且身份/target/causes绑定consent；取消/恢复失败不放行、不修改当前棋谱。Custom参数保真；更换引擎后旧query无权更新；结束后只读详情与save仍同一冻结值。generated details独立于个人C且C字节不变。
- **实际阻塞与未来证据门**: engine-rules能力/身份及Match owner；普通规则确认、Match准入/恢复和只读详情可按已冻结行为推进。仅generated details的保存/共享残余依赖`T03-MATCH-RULES-STORAGE-DECISION`结果；04#531选项演进交06合并。
- **具名决定 T03-MATCH-RULES-STORAGE-DECISION**（kind=`decision`，D，owner为规则保存责任人）：问题是如何保存/共享同一immutable match-rule snapshot且不写个人C、不建立ADR0003原范围禁止的第二生成文本通道。输入为ADR0003、GAME-05/SGF-05既有保存与重开合同、PR435冻结详情字段及04#531规则参数。输出一份明确的数据归属/结构化表示/兼容读取边界决定，逐字段说明保存、重开和共享的同值断言、未知字段处置及个人C字节不变；若需要改变现有ADR或产品可观察范围，记录逐项批准后才开放该save残余。停止于可执行且经适用批准的保存合同，不在本审计选择schema或实现产品。此决定不等待runner/policy，也不阻塞已冻结的准入、恢复或只读详情。
- **唯一批次建议 / 责任**: B/D（普通确认B；Match D）；`T03-MATCH-RULES-LIFECYCLE` 拥有此Delta的结果。具体合同按来源保留，同源内容去重不改变结果责任。
