## B58 — Bound exact restore path, quoting and incarnation admission

Current capability: [R12 — 运行生命周期、规则确认、恢复与身份隔离](../R12_PLAN.md#lifecycle). Historical source: `58-restore-path.md`.

### Capability result

Answer one bounded applicability/correctness question and stop with an evidence-backed disposition, not a speculative implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Question: Which supported local adapter actually sends an SGF filesystem path, and is it readable/encoded/GTP-safe for that engine incarnation?

Separate JSON position snapshot and GenericGTP replay/file restore. For file path consumers record physical filesystem/encoding/quote/reader identity and cleanup; unsupported virtual/wrapped path visibly typed-refuses before Ready or board mutation. If no such path exists, stop with no-path evidence rather than implement Java temp-file mechanics. GAME01 exactContinue original engine evidence is finite and must be cited, not extended.

Outcome must be one of precise unchanged/narrow inherited evidence, no corresponding reachable mechanism with source rationale, or named reachable gap with deterministic assertion and separately bounded successor. Do not implement a hypothetical Java defect here. Preserve original Accepted scope/candidate; no long stress run, arbitrary repeat/threshold matrix or architecture-immunity claim. Current supported paths can be assessed independently; a not-yet-built feature becomes that feature’s future evidence gate, not a blocker for current-path investigation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Path-sending/no-path adapter table names actual identity/quote/readability/cleanup boundary.
- Run replacement/old restore cannot write new board; unsupported path refuses visibly and keeps current state.
- Stop at path/adapter/evidence/residual table and a decidable conclusion; if a fix is needed, link its exact changed behavior and owner without implementing it in this investigation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

<a id="b60"></a>
## B60 — Bound restart reader/ACK/final authority transfer

Current capability: [R12 — 运行生命周期、规则确认、恢复与身份隔离](../R12_PLAN.md#lifecycle). Historical source: `60-restart-fence.md`.

### Capability result

Answer one bounded applicability/correctness question and stop with an evidence-backed disposition, not a speculative implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Question: Can admitted Restart share an old reader or numbered response/final and grant new owner authority before old retirement?

Record per-adapter Run/reader/numbered-response/final boundaries. Deterministic delayed old ACK/final after new Run cannot change Ready/capability/result. Compatible final engine gate from source#445 remains future evidence, not arbitrary rapid-click pass; broad adapter/runtime version changes invalidate old proof.

Outcome must be one of precise unchanged/narrow inherited evidence, no corresponding reachable mechanism with source rationale, or named reachable gap with deterministic assertion and separately bounded successor. Do not implement a hypothetical Java defect here. Preserve original Accepted scope/candidate; no long stress run, arbitrary repeat/threshold matrix or architecture-immunity claim. Current supported paths can be assessed independently; a not-yet-built feature becomes that feature’s future evidence gate, not a blocker for current-path investigation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Each supported adapter has restart teardown/owner-admission ordering and exact old-response assertion.
- Original evidence and final compatible-engine/native gaps are distinguished; no rapid-click stress substitute.
- Stop at path/adapter/evidence/residual table and a decidable conclusion; if a fix is needed, link its exact changed behavior and owner without implementing it in this investigation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

Source intents: `T03-LIFECYCLE-RESTART-FENCE-INVESTIGATION`.

<a id="b61"></a>
## B61 — Responsive bounded console output and load status

Current capability: [R12 — 首消费者诊断与响应式控制台](../R12_PLAN.md#diagnostics). Historical source: `61-console.md`.

### Capability result

Keep actual console output and load status responsive, identity-correct and bounded.

### Functional input scope

`B09`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

Ordinary scoped predecessors supply B09 applicable output budget, sanitizer and launch/Run identity result; source-answerable details and engineering representation are executor-owned, not unresolved product choices. Consume A's validated locale/resource-key/fallback result only. no complete diagnostics suite or installed support package is a prerequisite.

### User behavior and boundaries

Consume actual console/load consumer and diagnostics source budget, not unused Java EDT class. Bound stream buffering/render batching without dropping control/status/identity. Long/frequent stdout/stderr remains responsive to keyboard/scroll/Cancel; resource/load progress/error is visible and current. Existing command-input capability/safety/Match reservations do not broaden: no hidden commands, game mutation or implicit engine switching introduced by output presentation. Redact ordinary output/copy using shared sanitizer and do not reinterpret streaming as unbounded history.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Actual output/load entry shows current bounded stream/status/error with no stale Run attribution.
- Keyboard/scroll/Cancel remain responsive under bounded admitted output; memory/queue/source budgets have stated contract and no arbitrary timing promise.
- Visible/copy output redacts secrets and preserves existing console command guardrails and current game.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

## B65 — Verify supported local SGF/node exact position restoration

Current capability: [R12 — 运行生命周期、规则确认、恢复与身份隔离](../R12_PLAN.md#lifecycle). Historical source: `65-local-exact-restore.md`.

### Capability result

Answer one bounded applicability/correctness question and stop with an evidence-backed disposition, not a speculative implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Question: Do existing supported local import/selected-node adapters restore stones, to-play, komi and real move tail exactly?

This local investigation has no remote credentials/readboard/service prerequisite. Use frozen imported SGFs/selected nodes and actual admitted adapters: current scope square2–19, admitted rules/komi, empty root/PL, alternating moves/PASS, KataGo black-root handicap. Verify supported handicap, mainline forward/back and branch endpoints against actual engine position. White/mixed/midgame setup remains typed rejected before mutation; do not fakePASS/drop setup or port JavaSNAPSHOT topology. Unsupported input preserves current SGF/Run. READ02 external frame/save and GAME10 authoritative successors remain C-owned, not evidence for this local path.

Outcome must be one of precise unchanged/narrow inherited evidence, no corresponding reachable mechanism with source rationale, or named reachable gap with deterministic assertion and separately bounded successor. Do not implement a hypothetical Java defect here. Preserve original Accepted scope/candidate; no long stress run, arbitrary repeat/threshold matrix or architecture-immunity claim. Current supported paths can be assessed independently; a not-yet-built feature becomes that feature’s future evidence gate, not a blocker for current-path investigation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Actual compatible local engine/version/adapter and frozen SGF/node cases compare stones/to-play/komi/real tail.
- Unsupported setup/admission rejects without current game or existingRun mutation and support scope is not silently expanded.
- No problem records exact tested equivalence; actual difference produces bounded repair only after evidence.
- Stop at path/adapter/evidence/residual table and a decidable conclusion; if a fix is needed, link its exact changed behavior and owner without implementing it in this investigation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

Current capability: [R12 — PDA/WRN 成对只读回读](../R12_PLAN.md#runtime-readback). Historical source: `67-parameter-decision.md`.

### Capability result

Establish actual supported current-Run PDA/WRN readback commands/value domains and reader/round/numbered-response pairing evidence.

### Functional input scope

Capability-admission question: which named actual engine binary/version supports both readback commands, and what command/response evidence establishes valid finite PDA/WRN values for one current reader/Run/round?

B67 owns the minimal actual compatible local-engine/version protocol evidence before admission. Frozen readback/failure/edit-protection semantics are not a new product choice. Unsupported capability remains a named B68 admission gap; no new paired parameter writes, Apply transaction or write-lifetime policy is introduced.

### User behavior and boundaries

Bind PDA and WRN readback to current reader/Run/request round/numbered responses; both valid finite values are required for current pair success. Identify actual compatible engine binary/version, command/value domains and round retirement on cancel/reconnect/new Run from minimal real protocol evidence, not static argv or chart encoding. Default display is unknown until a valid current pair; invalidation preserves the last valid values with visible failed/unknown state. Current response must not overwrite unsent edits. Preserve existing editing behavior; this source contract neither pairs writes nor adds mutation/retry/reapply semantics.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

<a id="b68"></a>
## B68 — Paired PDA/WRN current-Run readback

Current capability: [R12 — PDA/WRN 成对只读回读](../R12_PLAN.md#runtime-readback). Historical source: `68-parameter-readback.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver current-reader paired runtime readback with truthful failure/unknown states and protection of unsubmitted edits.

### Functional input scope

`B67`, `B01`, `T02-I18N-FOUNDATION`

B67 minimal actual named-engine/version readback-capability evidence is not yet supplied; that precise protocol-admission gap, not product approval for new writes, keeps this feature needs-info. Consume B01 qualification and A validated locale/resource-key/fallback only. Pairing/failure/edit-protection behavior is already frozen; B68 owns native UI proof after capability admission.

### User behavior and boundaries

Use B67 admitted readback protocol result. Each round captures reader/Run/request/numbered-response IDs; only both valid finite current values publish a confirmed pair. Out-of-order arrivals can complete the correct round, but mixed old/new/error/non-finite/timeout or missing-half responses cannot claim success. Preserve the last valid pair with visible unknown/failure after invalidation, never fabricated zero. Last-valid runtime values and unsent editing state are separate; current readback never overwrites unsubmitted edits. Disconnect/reconnect/new Run retires the round with no silent retry. Existing editing behavior is preserved; no newly paired writes, Apply transaction or partial-write policy belongs to this scope.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Actual admitted engine readback publishes both parameters only for the exact current reader/Run/round/numbered-response pair; no new paired write/Apply operation is introduced.
- Out-of-order/error/non-finite/timeout/reconnect/oldRun maintain last valid+visibleunknown/failure and never combine rounds.
- Current readback does not overwrite newer unsubmitted edits; incompatible capability visibly refuses. B68 records native UI paired-success/failure/unknown and edit-protection proof using B67 admitted engine/version.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T05-PARAMETER-READBACK`.

Source intents: `T05-PARAMETER-READBACK`.

Parity references (historical scope, not promotion): `ANA-22`, `ENG-02`.

Delta sources: `UD-05-20`.

<a id="b72"></a>
## B72 — Verify local failed-handback→import→explicit Restart recovery

Current capability: [R12 — 运行生命周期、规则确认、恢复与身份隔离](../R12_PLAN.md#lifecycle). Historical source: `72-local-restart.md`.

### Capability result

Answer one bounded applicability/correctness question and stop with an evidence-backed disposition, not a speculative implementation.

### Functional input scope

None — existing baseline capabilities and the specifically scoped future evidence requirements below are inputs, not unpublished feature predecessors.

### User behavior and boundaries

Question: After task handback failure and another SGF import does explicit local Restart reconfirm new rules/position before user Continue?

No remote credentials/service/predecessor remote functionality is required. Use supported local engine chain: task return failure→new SGF import→explicitRestart→newreader/generation rules+positionconfirmation→Space/Continue. Preserve paused/failed gate until valid confirmation. Exercise failure/Cancel/another game/rules/engine change/late old callback with deterministic order; stale rules permission cannot unlock new game or publish old curve/move. No issue means record tested scope; only observed gap creates repair. Source UD05-25 remote-only stop/disconnect/reconnect/queued restart/external sync stays C separate real-service evidence, not loopback proof.

Outcome must be one of precise unchanged/narrow inherited evidence, no corresponding reachable mechanism with source rationale, or named reachable gap with deterministic assertion and separately bounded successor. Do not implement a hypothetical Java defect here. Preserve original Accepted scope/candidate; no long stress run, arbitrary repeat/threshold matrix or architecture-immunity claim. Current supported paths can be assessed independently; a not-yet-built feature becomes that feature’s future evidence gate, not a blocker for current-path investigation.

### Guardrails and non-goals

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

This is a bounded investigation, not implementation authority: output a disposition/evidence table or a separately approved narrow repair. Do not expand current protocol admission merely to make upstream examples pass.

### Observable acceptance

- Real supported local engine verifies new rules/position after explicitRestart before userContinue.
- Failure/Cancel/newgame/rules/engine/old callback cannot alter newRun/current game/Pause or unlock wrong confirmation.
- Local outcome is complete or actual local missing condition is named; remote chain has C responsibility and never blocks this one.
- Stop at path/adapter/evidence/residual table and a decidable conclusion; if a fix is needed, link its exact changed behavior and owner without implementing it in this investigation.
- Completion records actual start/final full SHA, materialized results, changed boundary, observed checks and exact engine/platform/resource conditions; unrun native/install/service/hardware surfaces remain named gaps.

### Owner/source mappings

Owner intents: `T05-LOCAL-IMPORT-RESTART-CHECK`.

Source intents: `T05-LOCAL-IMPORT-RESTART-CHECK`.

- Tauri映射/具名任务意图：本地 T05-LOCAL-IMPORT-RESTART-CHECK（B），受影响 ENG-02（Restart/Run）、ANA-16（任务）、ANA-06（持续分析）、SGF-01/SGF-07（导入/替换）；远程 T05-REMOTE-QUICK-HANDBACK（C），依赖本地所有权契约及 RCOMP-01，涉及 GAME-10 时另消费其确认契约。ANA-11不拥有自动快析。
- 覆盖证据或缺口：PR591的 `f360decb0440abfa82303ad9fe69cdd67a1070c5` 明确仅本地显式重启（`!targetEngine.useRemoteCompute`），不改变远程重连；`90afca6be97cd52245f815b1f1ffa89c137021e2`、`5b6a1333da8ec06e0fb219b70fbfd9e67bd9bda6` 保留比较退出/导入恢复交接。固定 Tauri lifecycle.rs:644–696 已在 Restart 退休旧任务并建立新 Run，document_departure.rs:418–490 拥有本地替换；这是调查起点而非新原生证据或已证缺陷。远程 PR584/587/592/595/596 中 stop response 先核对 reader binding；不复制 Java remoteGTP。
- 处置：本地已支持导入/显式 Restart/继续路径先做有界调查，不等 RCOMP 或远程账号；自动打开快析的新增部分与 T05-AUTO-QUICK-OWNER 串行整合。不得自动重启、自动恢复暂停意图或仅凭 Ready 清除局面确认。远程后继等本地任务所有权和远程协议稳定后接入，无真实服务证据不Passed。
- 后续验收/调查停止条件：本地受支持引擎验证任务交还失败→换谱→用户显式 Restart→新规则/位置确认→用户继续，以及失败、取消、又换谱/规则/引擎、迟到旧回调；新谱/Run/暂停意图不被旧结果污染，无问题记录所测边界，有问题才修复。远程另验真实端点停止/断线/重连、排队重启/取消与外部同步：无旧曲线/旧落子、不隐藏失败，不以loopback代替真实服务。

<a id="b73"></a>
## B73 — Approved explicit target-directed TensorRT resource repair

Current capability: [R12 — 合格资源、受管维护与显式修复](../R12_PLAN.md#resources). Historical source: `73-trt-repair.md`.

### Capability result

Deliver admitted TRT-directed repair or explicitly approved external-owner outcome while preserving original resource/user choice.

### Functional input scope

`B01`, `B04`, `B05`, `B02`

### User behavior and boundaries

This successor stays needs-info until B04 repair ownership, B05 qualification and B02 managed action results. If repair is admitted, only explicit user action checks/repairs original chosen TRT target even while non-TRT foreground runs. Revalidate selected resource identity and actual NVIDIA support, manifest/catalog integrity and trust before commit; unknown hardware does not pass. Failure/cancel leaves last-known-good resource and Ready A; stale repair cannot target new selection. Never fallback CUDA/OpenCL/profile or compile an engine. If the decisions assign external responsibility instead, close only with item-specific product approval and explicit accessible user guidance/qualified failure behavior; never a fake no-op repair button.

### Guardrails

Future implementation stays within Rust domain authority, Tauri gateway and TypeScript API-wrapper boundaries; preserve one engine-manager lifecycle, current-game/Match ownership, no implicit engine switch/restart, non-destructive failed/cancelled settings, and stale Run/job/document fencing. Permanent non-equivalent cuts require item-specific approval. ADR0001 single-series perspective, ADR0002 main-board heatmap, ADR0003 personal-C/generated separation, ADR0004 exact external successor for both GAME10 modes, and ADR0005 sole provider-authoritative Match remain fixed. Necessary visible errors/logs/redaction accompany each actual consumer; reuse B09's bounded diagnostics result where consumed, do not make all features wait for complete B10/export/install. A T02-I18N-FOUNDATION means only its validated resource-key/locale/fallback result, not completion of global search; map actual Java setting fields with the setting owner and leave full language/import closeout to F. No signed package, installed-path acceptance, new upstream compilation or release repository is a blanket feature prerequisite. Account/external writes never automatically retry.

### Observable acceptance

- Admission/ownership/hardware decisions are attached; known supported/unsupported/unknown target behavior is explicit.
- Actual admitted TRT target repairs transactionally under explicit action with identity revalidation and failure/cancel retention.
- Non-TRT foreground/changed selection/late repair and corrupt/trust/DLL failure do not retarget choice or damage Ready A.
- Missing actual GPU or compatible binary remains a named blocker, not CPU/fixture qualification; approved external responsibility has its own user-visible result.

### Owner/source mappings and frozen contracts

Owners: `T03-RESOURCE-TRT-REPAIR-INVESTIGATION`, `T03-RESOURCE-NVIDIA-HARDWARE-GATE`, `T01-RESOURCE`; source keys and full frozen behavior follow. This source contract consumes B05 hardware result, without replacing its separate investigation result or blocking B03 non-TRT maintenance.

#### T03-RESOURCE-TRT-REPAIR-INVESTIGATION

唯一owner `T03-RESOURCE-TRT-REPAIR-INVESTIGATION`；B；性质 investigation。

以UD-03-008的“实际阻塞与未来证据门”及“功能断言/调查停止条件”为完整合同；该意图拥有结果。结论前不派实现。

#### T03-RESOURCE-NVIDIA-HARDWARE-GATE

#### T04-RESOURCE

唯一owner `T01-RESOURCE`；B首个资源消费者；跨批共享合同；性质 功能增量（复用T01-RESOURCE；不复制别名）。

- **kind**: 功能增量（复用T01-RESOURCE；不复制别名）
- **batch / owner**: B；随受影响consumer提前；REL-05功能部分/ENG-01原profile边界；签名安装仍T01-RELEASE。
- **sourceDelta**: UD-04-001、UD-04-002、UD-04-005、UD-04-006、UD-04-008、UD-04-010、UD-04-014、UD-04-016、UD-04-018、UD-04-021、UD-04-024、UD-04-026、UD-04-028、UD-04-035、UD-04-037、UD-04-038、UD-04-043。
- **actual blockers**: 需要源/版本/路径/digest及兼容adapter能力的明确准入合同；可用合格本地资源。具体managed catalog/受控修复产品界面须先冻结；无生产Release/上游编译/签名开工前置。
- **bounded observable assertions**:
1. 原profile可继续用本地资源，不自动获取或切profile。目录资源按origin、source commit、backend、archive/executable与model/config身份验证；schema2的verified managed static-zlib例外只对catalog及manifest精确匹配且未修改的可执行文件适用。external/official/unknown不能借receipt去掉自身dynamic DLL合同，modified拒绝trusted-ready。
2. 15目标来源可枚举但按实际准入平台/硬件获取真实能力，不用CPU/fixture替GPU认证；源build、cloud transfer、JRE/installer实现不迁移。新版engineReleaseTag只改变来源联接，不证明已发布/URL可下载。离线/缺失/不兼容失败可见，既存current-game/ready A保留，autoload失败不fallback。
3. TensorRT repair只由明确用户动作触发，重新核对原选择目标/资源identity，不静默转CUDA/OpenCL；GPU提示不冒称真实GPU认证。保存/选择/刷新不隐式启动引擎。
4. B11-11750M默认变化记录实际采纳/未采纳原因与版本证据；不以default upgrade覆盖custom active selection。model/setup后台refresh失败不误enable，busy动作不可重入，旧snapshot不能覆盖当前identity。
- **验证环境/责任**: 以后用本地qualified binary/model、source fixture与真实已准入引擎/平台；组件签名/安装路径单独Not run。 该未来功能/调查实施者取得证据，阶段验收负责人观察并整合。
- **stop condition**: 每个上述resource子合同都有来源、失败断言与责任，consumer功能证据可引用；未准入平台保持有名缺口，不能用catalog存在结束。
